# SMSAndroidsPack: what players read, in Português (Brasil) (pt-BR)
# 
# Write the text in Português (Brasil) after each =, and leave the key before it as it is.
# The "# en:" line above each text is what the pack says now, whatever language that is in. The game uses a translation only while that is still what the pack says: when the pack changes a text, its old translation waits until somebody brings it up to date.
# Keep {words in braces}, <tags> and [TOKENS:like this] exactly as they are: the game fills them in or reads them.
# \n starts a new line. A line with nothing after the = shows the default text.
# To have an AI tool translate it, give it the whole file and ask it to translate only the text after each =, and to change nothing else: not the keys, not the notes, not the codes.
# In ModForge, File > Pack translations > Check the pack's translations says what is missing or damaged.
